## VOL-206 — Consensus & Disagreement

- Legacy implementation status: `implemented`
- Enterprise grade: `designed` → target `enterprise_qualified`
- Requirements: Keep independent evidence visible before aggregation. · Escalate unresolved high-impact disagreement instead of averaging it away.
- Capabilities: consensus proposals, dissent capture, resolution policy
- Contracts: `ConsensusProposal`, `Dissent`, `ConsensusDecision`
- Canonical surfaces: `skeleton/ai/agents`, `skeleton/eval`, `skeleton/ai/agents/consensus.py`
- Open obligations: define aggregation rules · bind reviewer independence

### L00 Intent & ownership

**Implementation**
- Consensus & Disagreement owns only its declared masterplan behavior; adjacent-plane behavior remains behind declared contracts.
- Owned capabilities: consensus proposals, dissent capture, resolution policy.

**Acceptance**
- All production entrypoints resolve to one canonical VOL-206 owner.
- Architecture validation rejects undeclared/shadow owners.

**Design invariants**
- Exactly one canonical production owner exists for the responsibility; compatibility facades may delegate but may not own policy, credentials, durable truth, or side effects.
- Declared capability scope is closed-world: undeclared behavior is rejected or routed through an explicitly declared adjacent-plane contract.

**Failure modes**
- Shadow-owner detection, duplicate durable authority, or competing policy evaluation is a release blocker.
- Ownership ambiguity during migration remains explicit transitional state with a single write authority and a dated removal/rollback plan.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- architecture_owner_resolution_failures_total = 0 at qualification
- shadow_owner_findings_open = 0 for production paths

**Required evidence**
- architecture/ownership validator output bound to exact head
- production entrypoint inventory proving canonical-owner convergence

### L01 Architecture & dependencies

**Implementation**
- Canonical implementation surfaces: skeleton/ai/agents, skeleton/eval, skeleton/ai/agents/consensus.py.
- Preserve canonical ownership, typed boundaries, bounded resources, deterministic fallback, exact-head evidence and rollback semantics.

**Acceptance**
- Dependencies are declared, governed and compatible with machine/architecture.json.
- Illegal/missing dependency fails before expensive work.

**Design invariants**
- Dependencies are explicit, versioned where externally visible, and cannot create a cycle that changes authority or startup order.
- Optional dependencies degrade capability explicitly; mandatory dependencies prevent readiness when unavailable or incompatible.

**Failure modes**
- Unknown dependency, incompatible version, or missing mandatory dependency fails before expensive/model/side-effect work.
- Partial dependency outage cannot silently convert an authoritative path into cache, projection, or compatibility fallback.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- dependency_resolution_failures by dependency and version
- capability-scoped readiness/degraded-state transitions

**Required evidence**
- dependency graph/fitness validation
- startup/degraded-mode tests for mandatory and optional dependencies

### L02 Contracts & compatibility

**Implementation**
- Primary contracts: ConsensusProposal, Dissent, ConsensusDecision.
- Security-sensitive serialization uses stable identity/versioning, canonical parsing, explicit absent/null behavior and migration semantics.

**Acceptance**
- Round-trip plus malformed/duplicate/unknown-value tests exist.
- Compatibility policy fails closed on unsafe ambiguity.

**Design invariants**
- Contract identity, schema version, canonical encoding, compatibility policy, absent/null semantics, and unknown-value behavior are stable and testable.
- Security-sensitive parsing rejects duplicate keys, non-finite values, ambiguous coercions, oversized fields, and unsupported versions before authority evaluation.

**Failure modes**
- Malformed or future-incompatible input fails closed with a stable normalized error and no side effect.
- Migration preserves identity/provenance and is reversible or explicitly reconciled when a perfect rollback is impossible.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- contract_decode_rejections_total by reason
- schema_migration_success/failure and compatibility-version telemetry

**Required evidence**
- round-trip, malformed, unknown-enum/version, duplicate-key and migration tests
- cross-runtime/canonical-serialization parity where multiple runtimes exist

### L03 Admission & authority

**Implementation**
- Keep independent evidence visible before aggregation.
- Attach principal/tenant, policy version, capability, budget, deadline and idempotency identity before protected work.

**Acceptance**
- Unauthorized/over-budget work causes no privileged side effect.
- Fallback never weakens privacy, residency, authority or tenant isolation.

**Design invariants**
- Principal, tenant/user binding, policy version, purpose, capability scope, budget, deadline, idempotency key, and data classification are known before protected work.
- Delegation can narrow but never widen authority; model output, retrieved content, tool results, network origin, and UI state cannot manufacture permission.

**Failure modes**
- Missing or ambiguous identity/policy/budget fails before provider/tool/state mutation.
- Fallback or retry reuses the original authority envelope and cannot relax privacy, residency, destructive-action, or tenant constraints.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- admission_denials_total by policy/budget/authority reason
- authority_decision_latency_p95 and stale_policy_rejections_total

**Required evidence**
- negative authorization/tenant/budget/deadline tests
- replay tests proving identical authority outcome for the same signed decision inputs

### L04 Runtime control flow

**Implementation**
- Escalate unresolved high-impact disagreement instead of averaging it away.
- normalize -> authorize -> reserve -> execute bounded capability -> persist/emit -> verify -> finalize receipt.

**Acceptance**
- Cancellation/deadline reaches every child operation.
- No retry/recursion/delegation/search/background loop is unbounded.

**Design invariants**
- Runtime flow is a bounded state machine with explicit terminal states, monotonic operation identity, cancellation propagation, retry budget, concurrency cap, and child-work ownership.
- A model may propose next work but deterministic orchestration owns admission, transition, side-effect dispatch, checkpoint, and finalization.

**Failure modes**
- Crash/restart resumes from durable checkpoint or reconciles ambiguity; it never guesses success from missing acknowledgements.
- Cancellation, timeout, or budget exhaustion stops descendant work and prevents new privileged I/O after terminalization.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- operation_duration_p50_p95_p99, cancellation_latency_p95, retries_per_operation
- orphan_work_detected_total, budget_exhaustions_total, terminal_state_conflicts_total

**Required evidence**
- state-machine transition/property tests including illegal transitions
- crash/cancel/deadline/retry/fan-out boundedness tests

### L05 State & data semantics

**Implementation**
- Classify Consensus & Disagreement data as authoritative, derived, cache or ephemeral with one owner.
- Durable changes use transaction/outbox or explicit idempotency/compensation; declared derived state is rebuildable.

**Acceptance**
- Acknowledged authoritative writes are not silently lost.
- Retention/deletion/migration/rebuild semantics are executable.

**Design invariants**
- Every record/store is classified authoritative, derived, cache, ephemeral, or recovery-only with one owner and explicit read/write semantics.
- Accepted durable mutations use transaction/outbox, compare-and-set/versioning, idempotency, or explicit compensation sufficient to make restart/replay semantics deterministic.

**Failure modes**
- Projection/cache failure cannot roll back accepted authoritative state unless the transaction contract explicitly couples them.
- Restore orders authoritative state before rebuildable projections; deletion/tombstones prevent resurrecting data outside lifecycle policy.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- authoritative_write_failures_total, outbox_backlog_age_p95, replay_reconciliation_total
- restore_rpo/rto, projection_lag_p95, deletion_propagation_lag_p95

**Required evidence**
- crash-before/after-commit and duplicate-replay tests
- backup/restore/rebuild/deletion/migration drills bound to authoritative state identity

### L06 Security, privacy & tenancy

**Implementation**
- Treat external/model/tool/retrieval/file inputs as untrusted until validated at the owning boundary.
- Threat/failure focus: false consensus | majority amplifies shared error.

**Acceptance**
- Critical authz/tenant/secret/injection failures are non-compensable.
- No undeclared protected material enters logs/prompts/artifacts/telemetry.

**Design invariants**
- Zero trust applies to user, model, retrieval, tool, file, network, plugin, provider, and worker input; validation occurs at the owning boundary and again at the last responsible moment before privileged effect.
- Tenant/user isolation, secret references, purpose limitation, residency, retention, egress, sandboxing, and least privilege survive caching, retries, derived artifacts, and fallback.

**Failure modes**
- Prompt injection, malicious retrieval/tool output, path/URL confusion, stale grants, cross-tenant identifiers, and secret-bearing payloads fail closed.
- Security/privacy/tenant regressions are non-compensable and cannot be approved merely because quality, latency, or cost improves.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- critical_security_boundary_violations = 0, cross_tenant_leaks = 0
- secret_redaction_failures = 0, denied_egress/authz attempts by reason

**Required evidence**
- adversarial injection/confused-deputy/tenant/secret/egress corpus
- threat-model-to-test traceability with all critical controls exercised

### L07 Failure, resilience & recovery

**Implementation**
- Open obligations: define aggregation rules | bind reviewer independence.
- Define timeout outcome, retry budget, circuit/bulkhead/backpressure, crash boundary, replay behavior and recovery checkpoint for Consensus & Disagreement.

**Acceptance**
- Fault injection covers dependency, crash, replay, saturation, cancellation, stale identity and rollback.
- Recovery creates no duplicate privileged effect or authority violation.

**Design invariants**
- Retries are bounded, jittered where appropriate, idempotency-aware, and combined with circuit breaking, bulkheads, backpressure, queue caps, deadlines, and explicit unknown-outcome handling.
- Recovery is deterministic from durable intent/checkpoint/evidence and stale workers/evidence cannot commit after fencing or source-identity invalidation.

**Failure modes**
- Dependency down/slow/partial, crash-before/after-commit, duplicate delivery, stale worker, split brain, saturation, cancellation, and rollback are injected as qualification scenarios.
- Unrecoverable or ambiguous state becomes durable reconciliation/incident work rather than silent success or blind retry.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- error_budget_burn, retry_amplification_factor, circuit_open_duration, queue_depth/age
- recovery_success_rate, recovery_time_p95, ambiguous_outcome_incidents_total

**Required evidence**
- fault-injection matrix covering every applicable architectural boundary
- recovery/replay/fencing tests proving no duplicate privileged effects

### L08 Observability & operability

**Implementation**
- Trace Consensus & Disagreement through decisions, dependencies, state, evidence and terminal result.
- Emit structured success/failure, p50/p95/p99 where applicable, queue/resource pressure, retries, cost and recovery telemetry with redaction.

**Acceptance**
- Failed operations are reconstructable without process-local memory.
- Telemetry overhead and redaction are tested.

**Design invariants**
- One operation identity correlates logs, metrics, traces, provider/model routes, retrieval/memory/tool calls, state writes, costs, evidence, approvals, and terminal receipts.
- Telemetry is structured, bounded, redacted, tenant-aware, sampling-aware, and never depends on exposing hidden reasoning content.

**Failure modes**
- Missing correlation, secret/PII leakage, unbounded cardinality, or telemetry-induced outage cannot be masked by functional success.
- Semantic health is distinct from transport/liveness health; stale green probes cannot authorize risky work.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- trace_reconstruction_coverage, telemetry_drop_rate, redaction_failures, cardinality_budget
- semantic_health_failures, SLO/error-budget series, telemetry_overhead_p95

**Required evidence**
- end-to-end trace reconstruction from user action to durable result
- redaction/cardinality/sampling/semantic-health regression tests

### L09 Performance, capacity & economics

**Implementation**
- Declare a conventional baseline with identical workload/environment identity before optimization.
- Reserve scarce resource/cost before expensive work; saturation uses bounded queueing/routing/shedding/rejection.

**Acceptance**
- Qualification includes tail and saturation behavior.
- Optimization dequalifies when source/policy/profile identity is stale.

**Design invariants**
- Performance/cost claims bind exact workload, source, model/provider, hardware, runtime, concurrency, cache/warm state, scorer, and resource budget.
- Admission reserves scarce compute/memory/storage/network/cost before expensive work; saturation degrades via bounded queueing, routing, shedding, deferral, or denial.

**Failure modes**
- Median-only wins, hidden warm-cache bias, oversubscription, OOM, noisy-neighbor starvation, thrash, or stale profile evidence invalidate optimization claims.
- Any optimized path that loses correctness/security/privacy/authority/tenant isolation dequalifies regardless of speed or cost.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- latency_p50_p95_p99, throughput_at_fixed_SLO, saturation_knee, queue_wait_p95
- cost_per_qualified_outcome, memory/compute/utilization, cache_hit_rate where applicable

**Required evidence**
- paired baseline/candidate load profile with tails and confidence/variance
- resource-pressure and dequalification/fallback tests

- operation_duration_p50_p95_p99, cancellation_latency_p95, retries_per_operation
- orphan_work_detected_total, budget_exhaustions_total, terminal_state_conflicts_total

**Required evidence**
- state-machine transition/property tests including illegal transitions
- crash/cancel/deadline/retry/fan-out boundedness tests

### L05 State & data semantics

**Implementation**
- Classify Adversarial Reviewer data as authoritative, derived, cache or ephemeral with one owner.
- Durable changes use transaction/outbox or explicit idempotency/compensation; declared derived state is rebuildable.

**Acceptance**
- Acknowledged authoritative writes are not silently lost.
- Retention/deletion/migration/rebuild semantics are executable.

**Design invariants**
- Every record/store is classified authoritative, derived, cache, ephemeral, or recovery-only with one owner and explicit read/write semantics.
- Accepted durable mutations use transaction/outbox, compare-and-set/versioning, idempotency, or explicit compensation sufficient to make restart/replay semantics deterministic.

**Failure modes**
- Projection/cache failure cannot roll back accepted authoritative state unless the transaction contract explicitly couples them.
- Restore orders authoritative state before rebuildable projections; deletion/tombstones prevent resurrecting data outside lifecycle policy.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- authoritative_write_failures_total, outbox_backlog_age_p95, replay_reconciliation_total
- restore_rpo/rto, projection_lag_p95, deletion_propagation_lag_p95

**Required evidence**
- crash-before/after-commit and duplicate-replay tests
- backup/restore/rebuild/deletion/migration drills bound to authoritative state identity

### L06 Security, privacy & tenancy

**Implementation**
- Treat external/model/tool/retrieval/file inputs as untrusted until validated at the owning boundary.
- Threat/failure focus: reviewer collusion | unactionable criticism.

**Acceptance**
- Critical authz/tenant/secret/injection failures are non-compensable.
- No undeclared protected material enters logs/prompts/artifacts/telemetry.

**Design invariants**
- Zero trust applies to user, model, retrieval, tool, file, network, plugin, provider, and worker input; validation occurs at the owning boundary and again at the last responsible moment before privileged effect.
- Tenant/user isolation, secret references, purpose limitation, residency, retention, egress, sandboxing, and least privilege survive caching, retries, derived artifacts, and fallback.

**Failure modes**
- Prompt injection, malicious retrieval/tool output, path/URL confusion, stale grants, cross-tenant identifiers, and secret-bearing payloads fail closed.
- Security/privacy/tenant regressions are non-compensable and cannot be approved merely because quality, latency, or cost improves.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- critical_security_boundary_violations = 0, cross_tenant_leaks = 0
- secret_redaction_failures = 0, denied_egress/authz attempts by reason

**Required evidence**
- adversarial injection/confused-deputy/tenant/secret/egress corpus
- threat-model-to-test traceability with all critical controls exercised

### L07 Failure, resilience & recovery

**Implementation**
- Open obligations: define reviewer prompt/contract | bind findings to repair workflow.
- Define timeout outcome, retry budget, circuit/bulkhead/backpressure, crash boundary, replay behavior and recovery checkpoint for Adversarial Reviewer.

**Acceptance**
- Fault injection covers dependency, crash, replay, saturation, cancellation, stale identity and rollback.
- Recovery creates no duplicate privileged effect or authority violation.

**Design invariants**
- Retries are bounded, jittered where appropriate, idempotency-aware, and combined with circuit breaking, bulkheads, backpressure, queue caps, deadlines, and explicit unknown-outcome handling.
- Recovery is deterministic from durable intent/checkpoint/evidence and stale workers/evidence cannot commit after fencing or source-identity invalidation.

**Failure modes**
- Dependency down/slow/partial, crash-before/after-commit, duplicate delivery, stale worker, split brain, saturation, cancellation, and rollback are injected as qualification scenarios.
- Unrecoverable or ambiguous state becomes durable reconciliation/incident work rather than silent success or blind retry.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- error_budget_burn, retry_amplification_factor, circuit_open_duration, queue_depth/age
- recovery_success_rate, recovery_time_p95, ambiguous_outcome_incidents_total

**Required evidence**
- fault-injection matrix covering every applicable architectural boundary
- recovery/replay/fencing tests proving no duplicate privileged effects

### L08 Observability & operability

**Implementation**
- Trace Adversarial Reviewer through decisions, dependencies, state, evidence and terminal result.
- Emit structured success/failure, p50/p95/p99 where applicable, queue/resource pressure, retries, cost and recovery telemetry with redaction.

**Acceptance**
- Failed operations are reconstructable without process-local memory.
- Telemetry overhead and redaction are tested.

**Design invariants**
- One operation identity correlates logs, metrics, traces, provider/model routes, retrieval/memory/tool calls, state writes, costs, evidence, approvals, and terminal receipts.
- Telemetry is structured, bounded, redacted, tenant-aware, sampling-aware, and never depends on exposing hidden reasoning content.

**Failure modes**
- Missing correlation, secret/PII leakage, unbounded cardinality, or telemetry-induced outage cannot be masked by functional success.
- Semantic health is distinct from transport/liveness health; stale green probes cannot authorize risky work.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- trace_reconstruction_coverage, telemetry_drop_rate, redaction_failures, cardinality_budget
- semantic_health_failures, SLO/error-budget series, telemetry_overhead_p95

**Required evidence**
- end-to-end trace reconstruction from user action to durable result
- redaction/cardinality/sampling/semantic-health regression tests

### L09 Performance, capacity & economics

**Implementation**
- Declare a conventional baseline with identical workload/environment identity before optimization.
- Reserve scarce resource/cost before expensive work; saturation uses bounded queueing/routing/shedding/rejection.

**Acceptance**
- Qualification includes tail and saturation behavior.
- Optimization dequalifies when source/policy/profile identity is stale.

**Design invariants**
- Performance/cost claims bind exact workload, source, model/provider, hardware, runtime, concurrency, cache/warm state, scorer, and resource budget.
- Admission reserves scarce compute/memory/storage/network/cost before expensive work; saturation degrades via bounded queueing, routing, shedding, deferral, or denial.

**Failure modes**
- Median-only wins, hidden warm-cache bias, oversubscription, OOM, noisy-neighbor starvation, thrash, or stale profile evidence invalidate optimization claims.
- Any optimized path that loses correctness/security/privacy/authority/tenant isolation dequalifies regardless of speed or cost.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- latency_p50_p95_p99, throughput_at_fixed_SLO, saturation_knee, queue_wait_p95
- cost_per_qualified_outcome, memory/compute/utilization, cache_hit_rate where applicable

**Required evidence**
- paired baseline/candidate load profile with tails and confidence/variance
- resource-pressure and dequalification/fallback tests

### L10 Verification & adversarial evaluation

**Implementation**
- Focused tests: skeleton/testing/test_blast_radius_alignment.py, skeleton/testing/test_human_control.py, skeleton/testing/test_autonomy_deescalation.py, skeleton/testing/test_agent_delegation_qualification.py.
- Evaluations: .github/workflows/p1-blast-radius-alignment.yml.
- Verification must map each masterplan requirement, declared risk, trust boundary, concurrency invariant, and recovery obligation to deterministic tests or immutable evaluation evidence; every discovered failure becomes a versioned regression before promotion.

**Acceptance**
- Happy, negative, adversarial, concurrency and recovery behavior are exercised.
- High-impact verification is independent or deterministically separated.

**Design invariants**
- Every requirement, risk, trust boundary, concurrency invariant, recovery obligation, and superiority claim maps to deterministic tests or immutable evaluation evidence.
- High-impact verification is independent in role/evidence path; evaluator identity, dataset/workload, scorer, environment, seed/config, and contamination state are versioned.

**Failure modes**
- Judge leakage, test contamination, flaky required gates, self-promotion, missing negative cases, or scorer drift invalidates promotion evidence.
- Discovered failures become durable regressions with stable identities before the repaired behavior can be promoted.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- requirement_test_coverage, adversarial_failure_rate, flake_rate, evaluator_agreement/calibration
- regression_escape_rate, qualification_reproducibility, benchmark_uncertainty

**Required evidence**
- focused unit/integration/property/fuzz/adversarial/recovery suites
- immutable evaluation bundle with baseline, candidate, raw results, statistical method and verifier verdict

### L11 Deployment, migration & rollback

**Implementation**
- Deployment binds exact source/config/contracts/data migration/dependencies/artifact provenance.
- Canary/readiness observes correctness, SLO, safety, cost and compatibility; rollback/reference fallback is rehearsed.

**Acceptance**
- No irreversible change lands without migration/reconciliation semantics.
- Rollback restores compatible authoritative state within target.

**Design invariants**
- Release artifacts are immutable and bind exact source, configuration, dependencies, contracts, schema/data migrations, SBOM/provenance, capability set, and enterprise evidence.
- Promotion proceeds through preflight, build, readiness, canary/smoke, monitored promotion, and rollback/failback criteria without hot production source mutation.

**Failure modes**
- Migration incompatibility, SLO/quality/safety/cost regression, provenance failure, or missing rollback target blocks or reverses promotion.
- Rollback preserves authoritative data compatibility or invokes explicit forward-reconciliation when data cannot safely move backward.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- deployment_success_rate, canary_regressions, rollback_success_rate, rollback_time_p95
- migration_duration/failures, provenance/SBOM coverage, release_evidence_freshness

**Required evidence**
- migration preflight + canary + rollback rehearsal on release-like topology
- artifact provenance/SBOM/signature and deployed-identity receipts

### L12 Operator controls & runbooks

**Implementation**
- Adversarial Reviewer exposes health/readiness distinct from semantic correctness plus bounded diagnostic/support evidence.
- Operator actions use preflight -> explicit admission -> execution -> immutable receipt; break-glass is scoped, expiring and audited.

**Acceptance**
- On-call can identify owner/state/dependency health/budget pressure/change/recovery action.
- Administrative actions cannot silently bypass policy/evidence.

**Design invariants**
- Operators have capability-scoped readiness, dependency health, saturation, recent-change, budget, queue, recovery, and evidence visibility without privileged-data leakage.
- Administrative and break-glass actions are explicit operations with scoped/expiring authority, preflight, two-person/approval policy where required, execution receipt, and postcondition verification.

**Failure modes**
- A green process health check cannot override semantic unready/degraded state; stale diagnostics cannot authorize destructive action.
- Support bundles and incident tooling remain tenant/redaction aware and cannot silently alter production state.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- MTTD/MTTR, operator_action_success/failure, readiness_transition_count, support_bundle_redaction_failures
- break_glass_uses, stale_health_rejections, incident_recurrence

**Required evidence**
- runbook/game-day exercising diagnosis, safe operator control, repair and rollback
- audit replay proving who authorized/executed what against which policy and state

### L13 Enterprise superiority & exit

**Implementation**
- Inherits common enterprise contract and must reach at least enterprise_qualified.
- Exit requires current exact-head evidence across correctness, security, tenancy, recovery, observability, performance/cost, compatibility and rollback.

**Acceptance**
- Target=enterprise_qualified; current=designed.
- Legacy status (implemented) cannot promote enterprise grade without dossier-conformant evidence.

**Design invariants**
- Enterprise grade is evidence-backed and monotonic only while governed source, contracts, policy, evaluator, workload, environment, and operating envelope remain current.
- A superior critical volume Pareto-dominates its declared baseline on required outcomes while meeting every non-compensable security/privacy/authority/tenant/state/recovery gate.

**Failure modes**
- Expired evidence, incomparable baseline, missing golden-journey coverage, open critical gap, or regression in a non-compensable dimension immediately invalidates enterprise/superior claims.
- Legacy implementation status, file count, LOC, prose, or self-scored benchmark can never substitute for qualification evidence.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- enterprise_qualification_freshness, dedicated_superiority_win_count, open_noncompensable_failures
- golden_journey_pass_rate, evidence_expiry/requalification_count

**Required evidence**
- exact-head enterprise dossier + superiority validator receipts
- applicable cross-plane golden-journey, comparator, rollback and independent-verifier evidence

**Evidence policy**
- Bind exact git head and governed source identity.
- Bind workload/dataset, model/provider if applicable, environment/hardware, policy/contracts, scorer, cost/resource budget, raw-result digest and verifier verdict.
- Expire evidence when governed source, contract, policy, evaluator or operating envelope changes.

**Completion rule:** Never sign from prose, file existence, LOC, happy-path tests or legacy implementation status; sign only after exact-head dossier requirements and enterprise gates pass.

---

## VOL-208 — Independent Verifier

- Legacy implementation status: `implemented`
- Enterprise grade: `designed` → target `enterprise_qualified`
- Requirements: Verifier must consume artifact-bound evidence and declared acceptance criteria. · Conflict-of-interest/identity rules must be machine-checkable where required.
- Capabilities: independent verification, evidence validation, signoff
- Contracts: `VerifierIdentity`, `VerificationTask`, `VerificationSignoff`
- Canonical surfaces: `skeleton/eval`, `machine/ai_build_accountability.json`, `skeleton/agents/blast_radius.py`, `skeleton/ai/agents/core/blast_radius.py`, `skeleton/agents/human_control.py`, `skeleton/agents/autonomy_control.py`, `skeleton/contracts/risk_evidence.py`, `skeleton/agents/__init__.py`
- Open obligations: bind identity separation · materialize verifier evidence checks

### L00 Intent & ownership

**Implementation**
- Independent Verifier owns only its declared masterplan behavior; adjacent-plane behavior remains behind declared contracts.
- Owned capabilities: independent verification, evidence validation, signoff.

**Acceptance**
- All production entrypoints resolve to one canonical VOL-208 owner.
- Architecture validation rejects undeclared/shadow owners.

**Design invariants**
- Exactly one canonical production owner exists for the responsibility; compatibility facades may delegate but may not own policy, credentials, durable truth, or side effects.
- Declared capability scope is closed-world: undeclared behavior is rejected or routed through an explicitly declared adjacent-plane contract.

**Failure modes**
- Shadow-owner detection, duplicate durable authority, or competing policy evaluation is a release blocker.
- Ownership ambiguity during migration remains explicit transitional state with a single write authority and a dated removal/rollback plan.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- architecture_owner_resolution_failures_total = 0 at qualification
- shadow_owner_findings_open = 0 for production paths

**Required evidence**
- architecture/ownership validator output bound to exact head
- production entrypoint inventory proving canonical-owner convergence

### L01 Architecture & dependencies

**Implementation**
- Canonical implementation surfaces: skeleton/eval, machine/ai_build_accountability.json, skeleton/agents/blast_radius.py, skeleton/ai/agents/core/blast_radius.py, skeleton/agents/human_control.py, skeleton/agents/autonomy_control.py, skeleton/contracts/risk_evidence.py, skeleton/agents/__init__.py.
- Zero-trust checks occur at the last responsible moment; identity, purpose, classification, residency and revocation follow protected work.

**Acceptance**
- Dependencies are declared, governed and compatible with machine/architecture.json.
- Illegal/missing dependency fails before expensive work.

**Design invariants**
- Dependencies are explicit, versioned where externally visible, and cannot create a cycle that changes authority or startup order.
- Optional dependencies degrade capability explicitly; mandatory dependencies prevent readiness when unavailable or incompatible.

**Failure modes**
- Unknown dependency, incompatible version, or missing mandatory dependency fails before expensive/model/side-effect work.
- Partial dependency outage cannot silently convert an authoritative path into cache, projection, or compatibility fallback.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- dependency_resolution_failures by dependency and version
- capability-scoped readiness/degraded-state transitions

**Required evidence**
- dependency graph/fitness validation
- startup/degraded-mode tests for mandatory and optional dependencies

### L02 Contracts & compatibility

**Implementation**
- Primary contracts: VerifierIdentity, VerificationTask, VerificationSignoff.
- Security-sensitive serialization uses stable identity/versioning, canonical parsing, explicit absent/null behavior and migration semantics.

**Acceptance**
- Round-trip plus malformed/duplicate/unknown-value tests exist.
- Compatibility policy fails closed on unsafe ambiguity.

**Design invariants**
- Contract identity, schema version, canonical encoding, compatibility policy, absent/null semantics, and unknown-value behavior are stable and testable.
- Security-sensitive parsing rejects duplicate keys, non-finite values, ambiguous coercions, oversized fields, and unsupported versions before authority evaluation.

**Failure modes**
- Malformed or future-incompatible input fails closed with a stable normalized error and no side effect.
- Migration preserves identity/provenance and is reversible or explicitly reconciled when a perfect rollback is impossible.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- contract_decode_rejections_total by reason
- schema_migration_success/failure and compatibility-version telemetry

**Required evidence**
- round-trip, malformed, unknown-enum/version, duplicate-key and migration tests
- cross-runtime/canonical-serialization parity where multiple runtimes exist

### L03 Admission & authority

**Implementation**
- Verifier must consume artifact-bound evidence and declared acceptance criteria.
- Attach principal/tenant, policy version, capability, budget, deadline and idempotency identity before protected work.

**Acceptance**
- Unauthorized/over-budget work causes no privileged side effect.
- Fallback never weakens privacy, residency, authority or tenant isolation.

**Design invariants**
- Principal, tenant/user binding, policy version, purpose, capability scope, budget, deadline, idempotency key, and data classification are known before protected work.
- Delegation can narrow but never widen authority; model output, retrieved content, tool results, network origin, and UI state cannot manufacture permission.

**Failure modes**
- Missing or ambiguous identity/policy/budget fails before provider/tool/state mutation.
- Fallback or retry reuses the original authority envelope and cannot relax privacy, residency, destructive-action, or tenant constraints.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- admission_denials_total by policy/budget/authority reason
- authority_decision_latency_p95 and stale_policy_rejections_total

**Required evidence**
- negative authorization/tenant/budget/deadline tests
- replay tests proving identical authority outcome for the same signed decision inputs

### L04 Runtime control flow

**Implementation**
- Conflict-of-interest/identity rules must be machine-checkable where required.
- normalize -> authorize -> reserve -> execute bounded capability -> persist/emit -> verify -> finalize receipt.

**Acceptance**
- Cancellation/deadline reaches every child operation.
- No retry/recursion/delegation/search/background loop is unbounded.

**Design invariants**
- Runtime flow is a bounded state machine with explicit terminal states, monotonic operation identity, cancellation propagation, retry budget, concurrency cap, and child-work ownership.
- A model may propose next work but deterministic orchestration owns admission, transition, side-effect dispatch, checkpoint, and finalization.

**Failure modes**
- Crash/restart resumes from durable checkpoint or reconciles ambiguity; it never guesses success from missing acknowledgements.
- Cancellation, timeout, or budget exhaustion stops descendant work and prevents new privileged I/O after terminalization.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- operation_duration_p50_p95_p99, cancellation_latency_p95, retries_per_operation
- orphan_work_detected_total, budget_exhaustions_total, terminal_state_conflicts_total

**Required evidence**
- state-machine transition/property tests including illegal transitions
- crash/cancel/deadline/retry/fan-out boundedness tests

**Failure modes**

- Restore orders authoritative state before rebuildable projections; deletion/tombstones prevent resurrecting data outside lifecycle policy.

- restore_rpo/rto, projection_lag_p95, deletion_propagation_lag_p95

- crash-before/after-commit and duplicate-replay tests
- backup/restore/rebuild/deletion/migration drills bound to authoritative state identity

- A green process health check cannot override semantic unready/degraded state; stale diagnostics cannot authorize destructive action.
- Support bundles and incident tooling remain tenant/redaction aware and cannot silently alter production state.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- MTTD/MTTR, operator_action_success/failure, readiness_transition_count, support_bundle_redaction_failures
- break_glass_uses, stale_health_rejections, incident_recurrence

**Required evidence**
- runbook/game-day exercising diagnosis, safe operator control, repair and rollback
- audit replay proving who authorized/executed what against which policy and state

### L13 Enterprise superiority & exit

**Implementation**
- Inherits common enterprise contract and must reach at least enterprise_qualified.
- Exit requires current exact-head evidence across correctness, security, tenancy, recovery, observability, performance/cost, compatibility and rollback.

**Acceptance**
- Target=enterprise_qualified; current=designed.
- Legacy status (implemented) cannot promote enterprise grade without dossier-conformant evidence.

**Design invariants**
- Enterprise grade is evidence-backed and monotonic only while governed source, contracts, policy, evaluator, workload, environment, and operating envelope remain current.
- A superior critical volume Pareto-dominates its declared baseline on required outcomes while meeting every non-compensable security/privacy/authority/tenant/state/recovery gate.

**Failure modes**
- Expired evidence, incomparable baseline, missing golden-journey coverage, open critical gap, or regression in a non-compensable dimension immediately invalidates enterprise/superior claims.
- Legacy implementation status, file count, LOC, prose, or self-scored benchmark can never substitute for qualification evidence.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- enterprise_qualification_freshness, dedicated_superiority_win_count, open_noncompensable_failures
- golden_journey_pass_rate, evidence_expiry/requalification_count

**Required evidence**
- exact-head enterprise dossier + superiority validator receipts
- applicable cross-plane golden-journey, comparator, rollback and independent-verifier evidence

**Evidence policy**
- Bind exact git head and governed source identity.
- Bind workload/dataset, model/provider if applicable, environment/hardware, policy/contracts, scorer, cost/resource budget, raw-result digest and verifier verdict.
- Expire evidence when governed source, contract, policy, evaluator or operating envelope changes.

**Completion rule:** Never sign from prose, file existence, LOC, happy-path tests or legacy implementation status; sign only after exact-head dossier requirements and enterprise gates pass.

## VOL-209 — Artifact Review Workflow

- Legacy implementation status: `implemented`
- Enterprise grade: `designed` → target `enterprise_qualified`
- Requirements: Preserve artifact version under review and review lineage. · Block publication/promotion until required reviewers/gates resolve.
- Capabilities: artifact review, review comments, repair/resubmission
- Contracts: `ArtifactReview`, `ReviewComment`, `ReviewDecision`
- Canonical surfaces: `skeleton/ai`, `skeleton/ai/artifact_review.py`
- Open obligations: define review state machine · bind to artifact registry

### L00 Intent & ownership

**Implementation**
- Artifact Review Workflow owns only its declared masterplan behavior; adjacent-plane behavior remains behind declared contracts.
- Owned capabilities: artifact review, review comments, repair/resubmission.

**Acceptance**
- All production entrypoints resolve to one canonical VOL-209 owner.
- Architecture validation rejects undeclared/shadow owners.

**Design invariants**
- Exactly one canonical production owner exists for the responsibility; compatibility facades may delegate but may not own policy, credentials, durable truth, or side effects.
- Declared capability scope is closed-world: undeclared behavior is rejected or routed through an explicitly declared adjacent-plane contract.

**Failure modes**
- Shadow-owner detection, duplicate durable authority, or competing policy evaluation is a release blocker.
- Ownership ambiguity during migration remains explicit transitional state with a single write authority and a dated removal/rollback plan.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- architecture_owner_resolution_failures_total = 0 at qualification
- shadow_owner_findings_open = 0 for production paths

**Required evidence**
- architecture/ownership validator output bound to exact head
- production entrypoint inventory proving canonical-owner convergence

### L01 Architecture & dependencies

**Implementation**
- Canonical implementation surfaces: skeleton/ai, skeleton/ai/artifact_review.py.
- Release is immutable/provenance-bound with migration preflight, canary, rollback, repair and compatibility evidence.

**Acceptance**
- Dependencies are declared, governed and compatible with machine/architecture.json.
- Illegal/missing dependency fails before expensive work.

**Design invariants**
- Dependencies are explicit, versioned where externally visible, and cannot create a cycle that changes authority or startup order.
- Optional dependencies degrade capability explicitly; mandatory dependencies prevent readiness when unavailable or incompatible.

**Failure modes**
- Unknown dependency, incompatible version, or missing mandatory dependency fails before expensive/model/side-effect work.
- Partial dependency outage cannot silently convert an authoritative path into cache, projection, or compatibility fallback.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- dependency_resolution_failures by dependency and version
- capability-scoped readiness/degraded-state transitions

**Required evidence**
- dependency graph/fitness validation
- startup/degraded-mode tests for mandatory and optional dependencies

### L02 Contracts & compatibility

**Implementation**
- Primary contracts: ArtifactReview, ReviewComment, ReviewDecision.
- Security-sensitive serialization uses stable identity/versioning, canonical parsing, explicit absent/null behavior and migration semantics.

**Acceptance**
- Round-trip plus malformed/duplicate/unknown-value tests exist.
- Compatibility policy fails closed on unsafe ambiguity.

**Design invariants**
- Contract identity, schema version, canonical encoding, compatibility policy, absent/null semantics, and unknown-value behavior are stable and testable.
- Security-sensitive parsing rejects duplicate keys, non-finite values, ambiguous coercions, oversized fields, and unsupported versions before authority evaluation.

**Failure modes**
- Malformed or future-incompatible input fails closed with a stable normalized error and no side effect.
- Migration preserves identity/provenance and is reversible or explicitly reconciled when a perfect rollback is impossible.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- contract_decode_rejections_total by reason
- schema_migration_success/failure and compatibility-version telemetry

**Required evidence**
- round-trip, malformed, unknown-enum/version, duplicate-key and migration tests
- cross-runtime/canonical-serialization parity where multiple runtimes exist

### L03 Admission & authority

**Implementation**
- Preserve artifact version under review and review lineage.
- Attach principal/tenant, policy version, capability, budget, deadline and idempotency identity before protected work.

**Acceptance**
- Unauthorized/over-budget work causes no privileged side effect.
- Fallback never weakens privacy, residency, authority or tenant isolation.

**Design invariants**
- Principal, tenant/user binding, policy version, purpose, capability scope, budget, deadline, idempotency key, and data classification are known before protected work.
- Delegation can narrow but never widen authority; model output, retrieved content, tool results, network origin, and UI state cannot manufacture permission.

**Failure modes**
- Missing or ambiguous identity/policy/budget fails before provider/tool/state mutation.
- Fallback or retry reuses the original authority envelope and cannot relax privacy, residency, destructive-action, or tenant constraints.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- admission_denials_total by policy/budget/authority reason
- authority_decision_latency_p95 and stale_policy_rejections_total

**Required evidence**
- negative authorization/tenant/budget/deadline tests
- replay tests proving identical authority outcome for the same signed decision inputs

### L04 Runtime control flow

**Implementation**
- Block publication/promotion until required reviewers/gates resolve.
- normalize -> authorize -> reserve -> execute bounded capability -> persist/emit -> verify -> finalize receipt.

**Acceptance**
- Cancellation/deadline reaches every child operation.
- No retry/recursion/delegation/search/background loop is unbounded.

**Design invariants**
- Runtime flow is a bounded state machine with explicit terminal states, monotonic operation identity, cancellation propagation, retry budget, concurrency cap, and child-work ownership.
- A model may propose next work but deterministic orchestration owns admission, transition, side-effect dispatch, checkpoint, and finalization.

**Failure modes**
- Crash/restart resumes from durable checkpoint or reconciles ambiguity; it never guesses success from missing acknowledgements.
- Cancellation, timeout, or budget exhaustion stops descendant work and prevents new privileged I/O after terminalization.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- operation_duration_p50_p95_p99, cancellation_latency_p95, retries_per_operation
- orphan_work_detected_total, budget_exhaustions_total, terminal_state_conflicts_total

**Required evidence**
- state-machine transition/property tests including illegal transitions
- crash/cancel/deadline/retry/fan-out boundedness tests

### L05 State & data semantics

**Implementation**
- Classify Artifact Review Workflow data as authoritative, derived, cache or ephemeral with one owner.
- Durable changes use transaction/outbox or explicit idempotency/compensation; declared derived state is rebuildable.

**Acceptance**
- Acknowledged authoritative writes are not silently lost.
- Retention/deletion/migration/rebuild semantics are executable.

**Design invariants**
- Every record/store is classified authoritative, derived, cache, ephemeral, or recovery-only with one owner and explicit read/write semantics.
- Accepted durable mutations use transaction/outbox, compare-and-set/versioning, idempotency, or explicit compensation sufficient to make restart/replay semantics deterministic.

**Failure modes**
- Projection/cache failure cannot roll back accepted authoritative state unless the transaction contract explicitly couples them.
- Restore orders authoritative state before rebuildable projections; deletion/tombstones prevent resurrecting data outside lifecycle policy.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- authoritative_write_failures_total, outbox_backlog_age_p95, replay_reconciliation_total
- restore_rpo/rto, projection_lag_p95, deletion_propagation_lag_p95

**Required evidence**
- crash-before/after-commit and duplicate-replay tests
- backup/restore/rebuild/deletion/migration drills bound to authoritative state identity

### L06 Security, privacy & tenancy

**Implementation**
- Treat external/model/tool/retrieval/file inputs as untrusted until validated at the owning boundary.
- Threat/failure focus: review targets old version | approval bypass.

**Acceptance**
- Critical authz/tenant/secret/injection failures are non-compensable.
- No undeclared protected material enters logs/prompts/artifacts/telemetry.

**Design invariants**
- Zero trust applies to user, model, retrieval, tool, file, network, plugin, provider, and worker input; validation occurs at the owning boundary and again at the last responsible moment before privileged effect.
- Tenant/user isolation, secret references, purpose limitation, residency, retention, egress, sandboxing, and least privilege survive caching, retries, derived artifacts, and fallback.

**Failure modes**
- Prompt injection, malicious retrieval/tool output, path/URL confusion, stale grants, cross-tenant identifiers, and secret-bearing payloads fail closed.
- Security/privacy/tenant regressions are non-compensable and cannot be approved merely because quality, latency, or cost improves.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- critical_security_boundary_violations = 0, cross_tenant_leaks = 0
- secret_redaction_failures = 0, denied_egress/authz attempts by reason

**Required evidence**
- adversarial injection/confused-deputy/tenant/secret/egress corpus
- threat-model-to-test traceability with all critical controls exercised

### L07 Failure, resilience & recovery

**Implementation**
- Open obligations: define review state machine | bind to artifact registry.
- Define timeout outcome, retry budget, circuit/bulkhead/backpressure, crash boundary, replay behavior and recovery checkpoint for Artifact Review Workflow.

**Acceptance**
- Fault injection covers dependency, crash, replay, saturation, cancellation, stale identity and rollback.
- Recovery creates no duplicate privileged effect or authority violation.

**Design invariants**
- Retries are bounded, jittered where appropriate, idempotency-aware, and combined with circuit breaking, bulkheads, backpressure, queue caps, deadlines, and explicit unknown-outcome handling.
- Recovery is deterministic from durable intent/checkpoint/evidence and stale workers/evidence cannot commit after fencing or source-identity invalidation.

**Failure modes**
- Dependency down/slow/partial, crash-before/after-commit, duplicate delivery, stale worker, split brain, saturation, cancellation, and rollback are injected as qualification scenarios.
- Unrecoverable or ambiguous state becomes durable reconciliation/incident work rather than silent success or blind retry.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- error_budget_burn, retry_amplification_factor, circuit_open_duration, queue_depth/age
- recovery_success_rate, recovery_time_p95, ambiguous_outcome_incidents_total

**Required evidence**
- fault-injection matrix covering every applicable architectural boundary
- recovery/replay/fencing tests proving no duplicate privileged effects

### L08 Observability & operability

**Implementation**
- Trace Artifact Review Workflow through decisions, dependencies, state, evidence and terminal result.
- Emit structured success/failure, p50/p95/p99 where applicable, queue/resource pressure, retries, cost and recovery telemetry with redaction.

**Acceptance**
- Failed operations are reconstructable without process-local memory.
- Telemetry overhead and redaction are tested.

**Design invariants**
- One operation identity correlates logs, metrics, traces, provider/model routes, retrieval/memory/tool calls, state writes, costs, evidence, approvals, and terminal receipts.
- Telemetry is structured, bounded, redacted, tenant-aware, sampling-aware, and never depends on exposing hidden reasoning content.

**Failure modes**
- Missing correlation, secret/PII leakage, unbounded cardinality, or telemetry-induced outage cannot be masked by functional success.
- Semantic health is distinct from transport/liveness health; stale green probes cannot authorize risky work.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- trace_reconstruction_coverage, telemetry_drop_rate, redaction_failures, cardinality_budget
- semantic_health_failures, SLO/error-budget series, telemetry_overhead_p95

**Required evidence**
- end-to-end trace reconstruction from user action to durable result
- redaction/cardinality/sampling/semantic-health regression tests

### L09 Performance, capacity & economics

**Implementation**
- Declare a conventional baseline with identical workload/environment identity before optimization.
- Reserve scarce resource/cost before expensive work; saturation uses bounded queueing/routing/shedding/rejection.

**Acceptance**
- Qualification includes tail and saturation behavior.
- Optimization dequalifies when source/policy/profile identity is stale.

**Design invariants**
- Performance/cost claims bind exact workload, source, model/provider, hardware, runtime, concurrency, cache/warm state, scorer, and resource budget.
- Admission reserves scarce compute/memory/storage/network/cost before expensive work; saturation degrades via bounded queueing, routing, shedding, deferral, or denial.

**Failure modes**
- Median-only wins, hidden warm-cache bias, oversubscription, OOM, noisy-neighbor starvation, thrash, or stale profile evidence invalidate optimization claims.
- Any optimized path that loses correctness/security/privacy/authority/tenant isolation dequalifies regardless of speed or cost.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- latency_p50_p95_p99, throughput_at_fixed_SLO, saturation_knee, queue_wait_p95
- cost_per_qualified_outcome, memory/compute/utilization, cache_hit_rate where applicable

**Required evidence**
- paired baseline/candidate load profile with tails and confidence/variance
- resource-pressure and dequalification/fallback tests

### L10 Verification & adversarial evaluation

**Implementation**
- Focused tests: skeleton/testing/test_artifact_review_workflow.py.
- Evaluations: skeleton/testing/test_artifact_review_workflow.py.
- Verification must map each masterplan requirement, declared risk, trust boundary, concurrency invariant, and recovery obligation to deterministic tests or immutable evaluation evidence; every discovered failure becomes a versioned regression before promotion.

**Acceptance**
- Happy, negative, adversarial, concurrency and recovery behavior are exercised.
- High-impact verification is independent or deterministically separated.

**Design invariants**
- Every requirement, risk, trust boundary, concurrency invariant, recovery obligation, and superiority claim maps to deterministic tests or immutable evaluation evidence.
- High-impact verification is independent in role/evidence path; evaluator identity, dataset/workload, scorer, environment, seed/config, and contamination state are versioned.

**Failure modes**
- Judge leakage, test contamination, flaky required gates, self-promotion, missing negative cases, or scorer drift invalidates promotion evidence.
- Discovered failures become durable regressions with stable identities before the repaired behavior can be promoted.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- requirement_test_coverage, adversarial_failure_rate, flake_rate, evaluator_agreement/calibration
- regression_escape_rate, qualification_reproducibility, benchmark_uncertainty

**Required evidence**
- focused unit/integration/property/fuzz/adversarial/recovery suites
- immutable evaluation bundle with baseline, candidate, raw results, statistical method and verifier verdict

### L11 Deployment, migration & rollback

**Implementation**
- Release is immutable/provenance-bound with migration preflight, canary, rollback, repair and compatibility evidence.
- Canary/readiness observes correctness, SLO, safety, cost and compatibility; rollback/reference fallback is rehearsed.

**Acceptance**
- No irreversible change lands without migration/reconciliation semantics.
- Rollback restores compatible authoritative state within target.

**Design invariants**
- Release artifacts are immutable and bind exact source, configuration, dependencies, contracts, schema/data migrations, SBOM/provenance, capability set, and enterprise evidence.
- Promotion proceeds through preflight, build, readiness, canary/smoke, monitored promotion, and rollback/failback criteria without hot production source mutation.

**Failure modes**
- Migration incompatibility, SLO/quality/safety/cost regression, provenance failure, or missing rollback target blocks or reverses promotion.
- Rollback preserves authoritative data compatibility or invokes explicit forward-reconciliation when data cannot safely move backward.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- deployment_success_rate, canary_regressions, rollback_success_rate, rollback_time_p95
- migration_duration/failures, provenance/SBOM coverage, release_evidence_freshness

**Required evidence**
- migration preflight + canary + rollback rehearsal on release-like topology
- artifact provenance/SBOM/signature and deployed-identity receipts

### L12 Operator controls & runbooks

**Implementation**
- Artifact Review Workflow exposes health/readiness distinct from semantic correctness plus bounded diagnostic/support evidence.
- Operator actions use preflight -> explicit admission -> execution -> immutable receipt; break-glass is scoped, expiring and audited.

**Acceptance**
- On-call can identify owner/state/dependency health/budget pressure/change/recovery action.
- Administrative actions cannot silently bypass policy/evidence.

**Design invariants**
- Operators have capability-scoped readiness, dependency health, saturation, recent-change, budget, queue, recovery, and evidence visibility without privileged-data leakage.
- Administrative and break-glass actions are explicit operations with scoped/expiring authority, preflight, two-person/approval policy where required, execution receipt, and postcondition verification.

**Failure modes**
- A green process health check cannot override semantic unready/degraded state; stale diagnostics cannot authorize destructive action.
- Support bundles and incident tooling remain tenant/redaction aware and cannot silently alter production state.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- MTTD/MTTR, operator_action_success/failure, readiness_transition_count, support_bundle_redaction_failures
- break_glass_uses, stale_health_rejections, incident_recurrence

**Required evidence**
- runbook/game-day exercising diagnosis, safe operator control, repair and rollback
- audit replay proving who authorized/executed what against which policy and state

### L13 Enterprise superiority & exit

**Implementation**
- Inherits common enterprise contract and must reach at least enterprise_qualified.
- Exit requires current exact-head evidence across correctness, security, tenancy, recovery, observability, performance/cost, compatibility and rollback.

**Acceptance**
- Target=enterprise_qualified; current=designed.
- Legacy status (implemented) cannot promote enterprise grade without dossier-conformant evidence.

**Design invariants**
- Enterprise grade is evidence-backed and monotonic only while governed source, contracts, policy, evaluator, workload, environment, and operating envelope remain current.
- A superior critical volume Pareto-dominates its declared baseline on required outcomes while meeting every non-compensable security/privacy/authority/tenant/state/recovery gate.

**Failure modes**
- Expired evidence, incomparable baseline, missing golden-journey coverage, open critical gap, or regression in a non-compensable dimension immediately invalidates enterprise/superior claims.
- Legacy implementation status, file count, LOC, prose, or self-scored benchmark can never substitute for qualification evidence.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- enterprise_qualification_freshness, dedicated_superiority_win_count, open_noncompensable_failures
- golden_journey_pass_rate, evidence_expiry/requalification_count

**Required evidence**
- exact-head enterprise dossier + superiority validator receipts
- applicable cross-plane golden-journey, comparator, rollback and independent-verifier evidence

**Evidence policy**
- Bind exact git head and governed source identity.
- Bind workload/dataset, model/provider if applicable, environment/hardware, policy/contracts, scorer, cost/resource budget, raw-result digest and verifier verdict.
- Expire evidence when governed source, contract, policy, evaluator or operating envelope changes.

**Completion rule:** Never sign from prose, file existence, LOC, happy-path tests or legacy implementation status; sign only after exact-head dossier requirements and enterprise gates pass.

**Design invariants**
- Retries are bounded, jittered where appropriate, idempotency-aware, and combined with circuit breaking, bulkheads, backpressure, queue caps, deadlines, and explicit unknown-outcome handling.
- Recovery is deterministic from durable intent/checkpoint/evidence and stale workers/evidence cannot commit after fencing or source-identity invalidation.

**Failure modes**
- Dependency down/slow/partial, crash-before/after-commit, duplicate delivery, stale worker, split brain, saturation, cancellation, and rollback are injected as qualification scenarios.
- Unrecoverable or ambiguous state becomes durable reconciliation/incident work rather than silent success or blind retry.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- error_budget_burn, retry_amplification_factor, circuit_open_duration, queue_depth/age
- recovery_success_rate, recovery_time_p95, ambiguous_outcome_incidents_total

**Required evidence**
- fault-injection matrix covering every applicable architectural boundary
- recovery/replay/fencing tests proving no duplicate privileged effects

### L08 Observability & operability

**Implementation**
- Trace Research Agent Team through decisions, dependencies, state, evidence and terminal result.
- Emit structured success/failure, p50/p95/p99 where applicable, queue/resource pressure, retries, cost and recovery telemetry with redaction.

**Acceptance**
- Failed operations are reconstructable without process-local memory.
- Telemetry overhead and redaction are tested.

**Design invariants**
- One operation identity correlates logs, metrics, traces, provider/model routes, retrieval/memory/tool calls, state writes, costs, evidence, approvals, and terminal receipts.
- Telemetry is structured, bounded, redacted, tenant-aware, sampling-aware, and never depends on exposing hidden reasoning content.

**Failure modes**
- Missing correlation, secret/PII leakage, unbounded cardinality, or telemetry-induced outage cannot be masked by functional success.
- Semantic health is distinct from transport/liveness health; stale green probes cannot authorize risky work.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- trace_reconstruction_coverage, telemetry_drop_rate, redaction_failures, cardinality_budget
- semantic_health_failures, SLO/error-budget series, telemetry_overhead_p95

**Required evidence**
- end-to-end trace reconstruction from user action to durable result
- redaction/cardinality/sampling/semantic-health regression tests

### L09 Performance, capacity & economics

**Implementation**
- Declare a conventional baseline with identical workload/environment identity before optimization.
- Reserve scarce resource/cost before expensive work; saturation uses bounded queueing/routing/shedding/rejection.

**Acceptance**
- Qualification includes tail and saturation behavior.
- Optimization dequalifies when source/policy/profile identity is stale.

**Design invariants**
- Performance/cost claims bind exact workload, source, model/provider, hardware, runtime, concurrency, cache/warm state, scorer, and resource budget.
- Admission reserves scarce compute/memory/storage/network/cost before expensive work; saturation degrades via bounded queueing, routing, shedding, deferral, or denial.

**Failure modes**
- Median-only wins, hidden warm-cache bias, oversubscription, OOM, noisy-neighbor starvation, thrash, or stale profile evidence invalidate optimization claims.
- Any optimized path that loses correctness/security/privacy/authority/tenant isolation dequalifies regardless of speed or cost.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- latency_p50_p95_p99, throughput_at_fixed_SLO, saturation_knee, queue_wait_p95
- cost_per_qualified_outcome, memory/compute/utilization, cache_hit_rate where applicable

**Required evidence**
- paired baseline/candidate load profile with tails and confidence/variance
- resource-pressure and dequalification/fallback tests

### L10 Verification & adversarial evaluation

**Implementation**
- Focused tests: planned:skeleton/testing/test_research_agent_team.py.
- Evaluations: planned:multi-agent research benchmark.
- Verification must map each masterplan requirement, declared risk, trust boundary, concurrency invariant, and recovery obligation to deterministic tests or immutable evaluation evidence; every discovered failure becomes a versioned regression before promotion.

**Acceptance**
- Happy, negative, adversarial, concurrency and recovery behavior are exercised.
- High-impact verification is independent or deterministically separated.

**Design invariants**
- Every requirement, risk, trust boundary, concurrency invariant, recovery obligation, and superiority claim maps to deterministic tests or immutable evaluation evidence.
- High-impact verification is independent in role/evidence path; evaluator identity, dataset/workload, scorer, environment, seed/config, and contamination state are versioned.

**Failure modes**
- Judge leakage, test contamination, flaky required gates, self-promotion, missing negative cases, or scorer drift invalidates promotion evidence.
- Discovered failures become durable regressions with stable identities before the repaired behavior can be promoted.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- requirement_test_coverage, adversarial_failure_rate, flake_rate, evaluator_agreement/calibration
- regression_escape_rate, qualification_reproducibility, benchmark_uncertainty

**Required evidence**
- focused unit/integration/property/fuzz/adversarial/recovery suites
- immutable evaluation bundle with baseline, candidate, raw results, statistical method and verifier verdict

### L11 Deployment, migration & rollback

**Implementation**
- Deployment binds exact source/config/contracts/data migration/dependencies/artifact provenance.
- Canary/readiness observes correctness, SLO, safety, cost and compatibility; rollback/reference fallback is rehearsed.

**Acceptance**
- No irreversible change lands without migration/reconciliation semantics.
- Rollback restores compatible authoritative state within target.

**Design invariants**
- Release artifacts are immutable and bind exact source, configuration, dependencies, contracts, schema/data migrations, SBOM/provenance, capability set, and enterprise evidence.
- Promotion proceeds through preflight, build, readiness, canary/smoke, monitored promotion, and rollback/failback criteria without hot production source mutation.

**Failure modes**
- Migration incompatibility, SLO/quality/safety/cost regression, provenance failure, or missing rollback target blocks or reverses promotion.
- Rollback preserves authoritative data compatibility or invokes explicit forward-reconciliation when data cannot safely move backward.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- deployment_success_rate, canary_regressions, rollback_success_rate, rollback_time_p95
- migration_duration/failures, provenance/SBOM coverage, release_evidence_freshness

**Required evidence**
- migration preflight + canary + rollback rehearsal on release-like topology
- artifact provenance/SBOM/signature and deployed-identity receipts

### L12 Operator controls & runbooks

**Implementation**
- Research Agent Team exposes health/readiness distinct from semantic correctness plus bounded diagnostic/support evidence.
- Operator actions use preflight -> explicit admission -> execution -> immutable receipt; break-glass is scoped, expiring and audited.

**Acceptance**
- On-call can identify owner/state/dependency health/budget pressure/change/recovery action.
- Administrative actions cannot silently bypass policy/evidence.

**Design invariants**
- Operators have capability-scoped readiness, dependency health, saturation, recent-change, budget, queue, recovery, and evidence visibility without privileged-data leakage.
- Administrative and break-glass actions are explicit operations with scoped/expiring authority, preflight, two-person/approval policy where required, execution receipt, and postcondition verification.

**Failure modes**
- A green process health check cannot override semantic unready/degraded state; stale diagnostics cannot authorize destructive action.
- Support bundles and incident tooling remain tenant/redaction aware and cannot silently alter production state.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- MTTD/MTTR, operator_action_success/failure, readiness_transition_count, support_bundle_redaction_failures
- break_glass_uses, stale_health_rejections, incident_recurrence

**Required evidence**
- runbook/game-day exercising diagnosis, safe operator control, repair and rollback
- audit replay proving who authorized/executed what against which policy and state

### L13 Enterprise superiority & exit

**Implementation**
- Inherits common enterprise contract and must reach at least enterprise_qualified.
- Exit requires current exact-head evidence across correctness, security, tenancy, recovery, observability, performance/cost, compatibility and rollback.

**Acceptance**
- Target=enterprise_qualified; current=designed.
- Legacy status (unverified) cannot promote enterprise grade without dossier-conformant evidence.

**Design invariants**
- Enterprise grade is evidence-backed and monotonic only while governed source, contracts, policy, evaluator, workload, environment, and operating envelope remain current.
- A superior critical volume Pareto-dominates its declared baseline on required outcomes while meeting every non-compensable security/privacy/authority/tenant/state/recovery gate.

**Failure modes**
- Expired evidence, incomparable baseline, missing golden-journey coverage, open critical gap, or regression in a non-compensable dimension immediately invalidates enterprise/superior claims.
- Legacy implementation status, file count, LOC, prose, or self-scored benchmark can never substitute for qualification evidence.

**Telemetry & SLOs**
- enterprise_qualification_freshness, dedicated_superiority_win_count, open_noncompensable_failures
- golden_journey_pass_rate, evidence_expiry/requalification_count

**Required evidence**
- exact-head enterprise dossier + superiority validator receipts
- applicable cross-plane golden-journey, comparator, rollback and independent-verifier evidence

**Evidence policy**
- Bind exact git head and governed source identity.
- Bind workload/dataset, model/provider if applicable, environment/hardware, policy/contracts, scorer, cost/resource budget, raw-result digest and verifier verdict.
- Expire evidence when governed source, contract, policy, evaluator or operating envelope changes.

**Completion rule:** Never sign from prose, file existence, LOC, happy-path tests or legacy implementation status; sign only after exact-head dossier requirements and enterprise gates pass.
